    const d = drag; drag = null; setCursor('default');
    if (d.on) { if (d.drop && e.type === 'pointerup') moveRowsTo(selRows(), d.drop.k, d.drop.g); }
    else { if (picks.size) { picks.clear(); pickSay(); } cur = { r: d.r, c: shownCol(0, 1) }; end = { r: d.r, c: N - 1 }; }
    placeTA(); ask(); return;
  }
  if (drag.a === 'edge') { COLS[drag.c].wSet = true; saveMine(); }   // 列の幅を覚える(このブラウザ・このタブ)
  if (drag.a === 'fill') { if (fillTo > rng().r2) fillDown(fillTo); fillTo = null; ask(); }
  if (drag.a === 'pan' && !drag.moved) go(drag.h.r, drag.h.c);
  if (drag.a === 'doc' && e.type === 'pointerup') { const h = hit(e.offsetX, e.offsetY); if (h.a === 'doc' && h.r === drag.r) openRecord(view[h.r]); }
  drag = null; placeTA();
}
cv.addEventListener('pointerup', up);
cv.addEventListener('pointerleave', clearTip);
cv.addEventListener('pointercancel', up);
cv.addEventListener('wheel', e => {
  e.preventDefault(); mark(e);
  if (popup) { const p = popup, vis = Math.min(p.items.length, 9); p.top = Math.max(0, Math.min(p.items.length - vis, p.top + Math.sign(e.deltaY))); ask(); return; }
  const k = e.deltaMode === 1 ? RH : 1;
  const dx = e.shiftKey ? e.deltaY : e.deltaX, dy = e.shiftKey ? 0 : e.deltaY;
  sx = Math.max(0, Math.min(maxSX(), sx + dx * k)); sy = Math.max(0, Math.min(maxSY(), sy + dy * k));
  if (editing) placeTA();
  ask();
}, { passive: false });

// ---- 他部署の同時入力の再現 ----
const sim = () => $('sim').checked;
const SIMT = ['3D1回目監修中(8/12戻し)', 'グレー原型2回目校了', '校了', '色味調整中', '依頼中'];
setInterval(() => {
  if (KT || !sim() || document.hidden || !view.length) return;
  const others = ['K', 'S', 'E', 'L'].filter(d => d !== dept);
  const d = others[Math.floor(Math.random() * others.length)];
  const cand = []; for (let c = 0; c < N; c++) if (COLS[c].dept === d && (c < F || (xOf(c) >= frozenR() && xOf(c) + COLS[c].w <= W - SC)) && COLS[c].w && ['date', 'st', 'sel'].includes(COLS[c].type) && !COLS[c].lock) cand.push(c);
  if (!cand.length) return;
  const c = cand[Math.floor(Math.random() * cand.length)];
  const first = rowAt(sy), last = Math.min(view.length - 1, rowAt(sy + bodyH()) - 1);
  const r = first + 1 + Math.floor(Math.random() * Math.max(1, last - first));
  const row = view[Math.min(r, view.length - 1)];
  const col = COLS[c];
  const v = col.type === 'st' ? SIMT[Math.floor(Math.random() * SIMT.length)] : col.type === 'sel' ? col.opts[Math.floor(Math.random() * Math.min(2, col.opts.length))] : parseDate('2026/12/' + (1 + Math.floor(Math.random() * 28)));
  mock.writeAs(`${DEPT[d]}の担当`, row.id, { [col.code]: v }); // 相手が保存した値は、次の定期読み込みで画面に入る
}, 2600);
function readNow() { lastRead = Date.now(); if (sync && (!TAB || TAB.ready) && !document.hidden && !pollIdle) sync.poll(); }
// 誰かが保存すると合図が届く。短い間の合図はまとめて1回読む
// EventSource はブラウザの同時接続の枠が詰まると黙って止まるので、枠の別な WebSocket で受ける
let db = null, dbOff = false;
function setSignal(v) { if (signalOn !== v) { signalOn = v; if (sync) saveStatus(sync.state()); } }
async function startSignal() {
  if (!KT || !KT.signal) return;
  const load = src => new Promise((ok, ng) => { const s = document.createElement('script'); s.src = src; s.onload = ok; s.onerror = ng; document.head.append(s); });
  try {
    for (const f of ['app', 'database']) await load(`https://www.gstatic.com/firebasejs/10.14.1/firebase-${f}-compat.js`);
  } catch (e) { console.warn('合図を受ける部品を読めませんでした', e); return; } // 定期読み込みのまま続ける
  db = firebase.initializeApp({ databaseURL: KT.signal.url }).database();
  db.ref('.info/connected').on('value', s => setSignal(s.val() === true));
  db.ref(KT.signal.path).on('value', s => {
    const d = s.val();
    if (d && d.deleted) dropRow(d.deleted);
    clearTimeout(signalT); signalT = setTimeout(readNow, 200);
  });
}
// 裏のタブと放置中の画面はつなぎを切る
function listenSignal() {
  const off = document.hidden || pollIdle;
  if (!db || off === dbOff) return;
  dbOff = off;
  if (off) db.goOffline(); else db.goOnline();
}
startSignal();
// 操作している間だけ読み込む。戻ってきたらすぐ読み直す
setInterval(() => {
  const idle = Date.now() - lastActive > IDLE_MS;
  if (idle !== pollIdle) { pollIdle = idle; if (sync) saveStatus(sync.state()); }
  listenSignal();
  if (Date.now() - lastRead >= (signalOn ? SAFE_MS : POLL_MS)) readNow();
}, 3000);
for (const t of ['keydown', 'pointerdown', 'pointermove', 'wheel']) window.addEventListener(t, () => { lastActive = Date.now(); }, { capture: true, passive: true });
const wake = () => { lastActive = Date.now(); pollIdle = false; listenSignal(); if (sync && !document.hidden) sync.poll(); };
window.addEventListener('focus', wake);
document.addEventListener('visibilitychange', wake);
window.addEventListener('beforeunload', e => { if (sync && sync.state().waiting) e.preventDefault(); });

} catch (_) {}

// 起動
layout(); readTheme();
async function start() {
  if (KT) {
    for (const id of ['dept', 'rows', 'sim', 'off', 'same']) $(id).closest('label, button').style.display = 'none';
    $('bar').querySelector('b').textContent = KT.test ? '試作' : '進捗シート';
    $('bar').querySelector('.note').textContent = `${KT.test ? `【DEV】アプリ${KT.app}に直接保存します。` : '入力すると自動で保存します。'}誰がいつ変えたかはキントーンの変更履歴に残ります。`;
    setupTools();
    if (KT.embedded) {
      const b = $('fullBtn'), label = () => { b.textContent = KT.full() ? '全画面を解除' : '全画面表示'; };
      b.style.cursor = 'pointer';
      const toggle = () => { KT.full(!KT.full()); label(); ta.focus({ preventScroll: true }); };
      b.addEventListener('click', toggle);
      label();
      // 全画面で隠れるキントーンの操作をここから使う。ヘッダーのボタンは本物を押す
      const m = $('menuBtn'), name = () => { m.textContent = KT.view ? `メニュー(${KT.view.name})` : 'メニュー'; };
      m.style.display = ''; KT.showView = name;
      const switchView = async v => {
        KT.view = v;
        try { localStorage.setItem('progressSheetView', v.name); } catch (_) {}
        colFilter.clear(); saveMine();
        await reloadAll();
        if (view.length) go(0, cur.c);
      };
      m.addEventListener('click', async () => {
        const head = KT.kintone.app.getHeaderMenuSpaceElement();
        const items = [
          { label: 'ホーム', url: '/k/#/portal' },
          ...(KT.views || []).map(v => ({ label: `${v === KT.view ? '✓' : '　'} 一覧: ${v.name}`, run: () => switchView(v) })),
          ...(head ? [...head.querySelectorAll('button')] : []).map(x => ({ label: x.textContent.trim(), run: () => x.click() })),
          { label: KT.full() ? '全画面を解除' : '全画面表示', run: toggle },
        ];
        openPopup(m.getBoundingClientRect().left, 0, 200, items, 0, it => { if (it.url) parent.location.href = it.url; else it.run(); });
        if (popQ.hidden) ta.focus({ preventScroll: true });
      });
    }
    if (!KT.embedded) {
      const close = document.createElement('button');
      close.type = 'button'; close.textContent = '閉じる';
      close.addEventListener('click', () => { if (sync.state().waiting) say('保存が終わってから閉じてください'); else KT.close(); });
      $('bar').appendChild(close);
    }
  }
  $('toolMine').style.cursor = 'pointer'; $('toolMine').addEventListener('click', openMine);
  for (const [id, run] of [['toolMeRows', toggleRows], ['toolMeCols', toggleCols], ['toolGroup', () => showGroupMaker()], ['toolColor', togglePalette], ['toolLook', toggleLook],['toolUndo', () => { undo(undoS, redoS, 'old'); ta.focus({ preventScroll: true }); }], ['toolRedo', () => { undo(redoS, undoS, 'v'); ta.focus({ preventScroll: true }); }]]) { $(id).style.cursor = 'pointer'; $(id).addEventListener('click', run); }
  // フィギュア進捗モードの切り替え(フィギュアのタブだけ)。タブを切り替えるときと同じく、保存を待ってから開き直す
  if (TAB && TAB.name === FIG_TAB) {
    $('figModeGrp').style.display = '';
    $('toolFigMode').classList.toggle('on', FIG_MODE); $('toolFigMode').style.cursor = 'pointer';
    $('toolFigMode').addEventListener('click', async () => {
      if (sync) { await sync.flush(); if (sync.state().waiting) return say('保存が終わっていないセルがあります。通信が戻ってから切り替えてください', 8000); }
      if (!refreshing) await snapSave();
      try { localStorage.setItem(FIG_MODE_KEY, FIG_MODE ? '0' : '1'); } catch (_) {}
      location.reload();
    });
  }
  // CAPSモードの切り替え(カプセル・BOXTOY のタブだけ)。PALモードと同じく、保存を待ってから開き直す
  if (TAB && CAPS_TABS.includes(TAB.name)) {
    $('figModeGrp').style.display = ''; $('toolFigMode').style.display = 'none';
    const cb = $('toolCapsMode'); cb.style.display = ''; cb.classList.toggle('on', CAPS_MODE); cb.style.cursor = 'pointer';
    cb.addEventListener('click', async () => {
      if (sync) { await sync.flush(); if (sync.state().waiting) return say('保存が終わっていないセルがあります。通信が戻ってから切り替えてください', 8000); }
      if (!refreshing) await snapSave();
      try { localStorage.setItem(CAPS_MODE_KEY, CAPS_MODE ? '0' : '1'); } catch (_) {}
      location.reload();
    });
  }
  setupTabs();
  const snap = KT && TAB ? await snapGet() : null;
  if (snap) say('前に開いたときの内容を出しています。最新を確かめています…', 8000);
  rows = KT ? await loadKintone(snap) : TAB ? [] : makeRows(2000);
  if (KT && TAB && !snap) setTimeout(snapSave, 0);
  if (TAB && !TAB.ready || CAPS_MODE) for (const id of ['toolAdd', 'toolCopy']) $(id).className = 'off';
  connect(); loadMine(); loadStaff();
  W = wrap.clientWidth; H = wrap.clientHeight;
  buildView(); resize();
  ta.focus({ preventScroll: true });
  let jump = ''; try { jump = sessionStorage.getItem(XS_JUMP) || ''; sessionStorage.removeItem(XS_JUMP); } catch (_) {}
  if (jump) focusRow(jump);
  if (snap) refreshFromServer();
  if (KT && TAB) setTimeout(prewarmTabs, 2500);
  if (KT) setTimeout(janWarnCheck, 1200);
  setTimeout(() => { window.__ready = true; pf.stall = 0; pf.stallMax = 0; pf.latMax = 0; pf.drawMax = 0; ask(); }, 1500);
}
start().catch(e => say(`キントーンを読めませんでした: ${e.message || e.code}`, 60000));
window.__t = { pf, draw, go, apply, colOf, get sync() { return sync; }, get mock() { return mock; }, get saveState() { return saveState; }, get msg() { return msg; }, get changes() { return changes; }, get view() { return view; }, get editing() { return editing; }, get cur() { return cur; }, scroll(y, x) { sy = y; sx = x; }, rng };
</script>
